(needs_source: the verses are unattributed) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:24:30}
- He says the heart is a vessel and the tongue a ladle that takes from it, so whoever's heart is full of good is heard to speak good. (needs_source: the saying is unattributed) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:25:30}
- He says a smile is a fishing rod that catches hearts, relates by sense that the Prophet would smile until his molars showed, and says he gathered a hundred and twenty situations of the Prophet's joy and grief and found that he rejoiced and grieved only for religion, then contrasts our joy and grief over this world. (needs_source: the report is by sense and the count has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:27:00}
- He says what one should rejoice at is a person who stays on the path, leaves bribery and lowers his gaze, and not at worldly posts. This is his opinion. (needs_source: part of the passage is a repeated loop) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:28:00}
- He says a person has a human devil and a jinn devil, that the human devil is stronger because God put it first, and that the two protective suras drive off the jinn devil while the human devil interprets them. See [[passage/quran-113-1|al-Falaq, verse 1]] and [[passage/quran-114-1|al-Nas, verse 1]]. (needs_source: his statement on the order and on the two suras is his own interpretation) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:29:30}
- He tells of a righteous man who set out with a sack of flour as alms for a widow and whose wife said his own children came first, and when asked he said he had beaten seventy devils and their mother beat him; he cites that some spouses and children are enemies. See [[passage/quran-64-14|al-Taghabun, verse 14]]. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:31:00}
- He says household demands may push a father to unlawful earning and that one who aims at this world is never satisfied. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:31:30}
- He says the heart lives by remembering God, the Qur'an and eating lawful food, that a sick heart left untreated becomes dead, and likens it to the cat that knows what it snatched is not its own. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:32:30}
- He says the word halal comes from an open mouth and haram from a closed one, and that the lawful is in the light and the forbidden in the dark. (needs_source: the derivation has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:32:30}
- He likens people to the bee, which settles only on what is good, and the fly, which settles on wounds; he criticises tracing the news and scandals of celebrities and says a person will not enter the grave with anyone else and will be reckoned alone. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:34:30}
- He advises putting the heart on the scale by asking whether one is among those who rely on God, the truthful and the doers of good, or among the hypocrites who praise a manager for what they do not believe. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:36:00}
- He lists among the traits of the servants of the Merciful walking humbly, answering the ignorant with peace, spending the night in prostration and standing, and passing by idle talk with dignity. See [[passage/quran-25-63|al-Furqan, verse 63]], [[passage/quran-25-64|al-Furqan, verse 64]] and [[passage/quran-25-72|al-Furqan, verse 72]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:37:00}
- He says time cannot be bought and is the most precious thing a person has, and that on the Day of Resurrection the guilty will swear they stayed but an hour. See [[passage/quran-30-55|al-Rum, verse 55]]. (needs_source: part of the passage is a repeated loop) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:37:30}
- He closes by saying that one who wants to escape the people of the blaze must make his heart sound. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep06 00:37:30}

## Episode 7: the opening of the breast to Islam and the words I, mine and I have
- He pauses at the verse of the day and says there are two kinds of people: those who take the path of guidance, who are few, and the majority who neither believe nor give thanks. See [[passage/quran-6-125|al-An'am, verse 125]], [[passage/quran-12-103|Yusuf, verse 103]] and [[passage/quran-34-13|Saba, verse 13]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:02:00}
- He says the "mala'", the elite around the ruler, are usually those who resist uprightness, and notes from the spelling and case ending of the word that the mala' of Bilqis and Solomon are respected and consulted while Pharaoh's has no worth, and that God sent two messengers to Pharaoh but one to each of the other peoples. (needs_source: his remarks on spelling and grammar are his own reasoning without a source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:04:00}
- He says Pharaoh made light of his people and they obeyed him, and generalises that the Egyptians cling to the leader. This is his opinion and generalisation. See [[passage/quran-43-54|al-Zukhruf, verse 54]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:04:30}
- He relates by sense that the Prophet forbade being a yes-man, so that people do good if others do good and avoid their wrongs if they do wrong, and says one follows the Lord of people and not the people. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:05:30}
- He says a person has two doors, a door of guidance and a door of misguidance, that God left the choice to him, and that one whose breast God opens hears good counsel and applies it. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:06:00}
- He distinguishes biological sleep from civilisational sleep, says the community has slept about four hundred years, that the door of ijtihad was closed from the seventh century AH, that books of jurisprudence still use the sa', mudd and farsakh, and calls for renewing religious discourse in the sense of suiting speech to the situation of those addressed. This is his opinion on a disputed matter. (needs_source: his dating of the closing of ijtihad has no source and the following passage is a repeated loop) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:07:30}
- He warns against arrogance with knowledge and says in every field there is someone who knows more than you. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:08:00}
- He says God described Pharaoh as exalted in the earth and not in heaven, that he is high inside himself, and compares the division of societies into classes, such as blue blood in the West and the five classes of the Romans. See [[passage/quran-28-4|al-Qasas, verse 4]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:09:30}
- He relates by sense that the Prophet said you are all from Adam and Adam is from dust. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:09:30}
- He tells that the Prophet passed people pollinating palms and suggested a method, the palms did not bear fruit, and he said you know better the affairs of your world; he says the Prophet is protected in what concerns God's law and a human being in other things, and relates by sense that whoever turns from his Sunna is not of him. (needs_source: the story and the report are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:10:30}
- He says three words enter every sentence we say, I, mine and I have, and that overusing them needs psychological, religious and social treatment. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:11:30}
- He mentions Iblis saying he is better than Adam, created of fire against clay, and being expelled from God's mercy, says people follow Iblis who caused their father's exit from Paradise, and gives the example of fashion that people follow each season. See [[passage/quran-7-12|al-A'raf, verse 12]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:13:00}
- He mentions the trust offered and borne by man, described as unjust and ignorant, explains injustice as putting a thing in the wrong place, and gives the examples of a husband not just to his wife, a wife frowning at her husband while smiling at guests, and an official who favours the hypocrite and jails the frank adviser. See [[passage/quran-33-72|al-Ahzab, verse 72]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:15:00}
- He says Pharaoh said "mine" when he spoke of the kingdom of Egypt and the rivers flowing beneath him and asked Haman for a tower to reach Moses' God, whom he thought a liar. See [[passage/quran-43-51|al-Zukhruf, verse 51]] and [[passage/quran-28-38|al-Qasas, verse 38]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:16:00}
- He mentions Pharaoh's saying that he feared Moses would change their religion or spread corruption in the land, says the arrogant dislike advice, and gives the example of a man who rebukes someone standing out of line at tarawih. See [[passage/quran-40-26|Ghafir, verse 26]] and [[passage/quran-61-4|al-Saff, verse 4]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:17:00}
- He says there is no hadith ordering a crooked row to be straightened or threatening it in these words, and that some scholars say it as a reminder. (needs_source: his statement that the hadith is not established has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:17:30}
- He says Qarun said "I have" in his saying that he was given this because of knowledge he had, and sums up that Iblis was expelled, Pharaoh drowned and Qarun was swallowed by the earth. See [[passage/quran-28-78|al-Qasas, verse 78]] and [[passage/quran-28-81|al-Qasas, verse 81]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:18:00}
- He says he wants human minds that accept the truth and oppose desire, and that the Prophet does not love the backbiter, the tale-bearer, the liar, the one who does not pray, the unjust or the killer, but loves the truthful, the upright, the rememberer of God, the one who relies on Him and the one who does good to people. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:19:30}
- He relates by sense that a woman entered the Fire because of a cat she shut up, neither feeding it nor letting it eat the creatures of the earth, and then says some rulers imprisoned and killed thousands and that al-Hajjaj killed a hundred and twenty thousand. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain, and the figure has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:20:30}
- He warns against injustice as darkness on the Day of Resurrection, tells whoever is tempted by his power to remember God's power over him, and mentions that the Children of Israel were told if you do good you do it for yourselves and if you do evil it is against yourselves. See [[passage/quran-17-4|al-Isra, verse 4]] and [[passage/quran-17-7|al-Isra, verse 7]]. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:21:30}
- He pauses at the order of the verses of al-Rahman, teaching the Qur'an, creating man and teaching clear speech, and says one who has not learned the Qur'an and applied it is as if he had not been created. This is his interpretation. See [[passage/quran-55-1|al-Rahman, verse 1]], [[passage/quran-55-2|al-Rahman, verse 2]] and [[passage/quran-55-3|al-Rahman, verse 3]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:23:00}
- He tells that a servant poured water for Harun al-Rashid and the jug wounded his hand, and the servant said successive phrases of the verse on restraining anger, pardoning people and God loving the good-doers, and Harun freed him; he draws that kindness returns to the doer. See [[passage/quran-3-134|Al Imran, verse 134]]. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:25:00}
- He says greatness belongs to God alone, and tells that a delegation asked for the palace of the Commander of the Faithful Umar and was shown a modest house, and found him asleep on his back under a tree with his hand under his head, and quotes lines of Hafiz Ibrahim that he judged justly, so was safe and slept. See [[passage/quran-74-3|al-Muddaththir, verse 3]]. (needs_source: the story has no chain, and the ascription of the lines is his) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:29:00}
- He criticises pride in lineage and says a person is the son of dust and clay and precious to God if he stays upright. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:28:00}
- He reports the objection that the veil is the veil of the heart, and says an unveiled woman thinks herself free while the veiled woman is the free one because she knows her permanent and temporary mahram and the stranger. This is his opinion on a disputed matter. (needs_source: part of the passage is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:34:00}
- He says whoever invests in the hereafter is honoured, describes Paradise as a place where a rider travels a thousand years and whose youths are like scattered pearls, and stresses that this does not stop work in this world, mentioning that David ate from the work of his hand. See [[passage/quran-3-133|Al Imran, verse 133]] and [[passage/quran-76-19|al-Insan, verse 19]]. (needs_source: the thousand-year journey and the report on David have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:32:00}
- He says young people think religion is chains of lowering the gaze, holding the tongue and giving up songs and films, and holds that whoever loves God's Book is loved by God and comes to love the law, so the chains are on the one far from it. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:32:30}
- He takes up dress for men and women at work and says we look unrealistically, accusing a man who covers his private parts of madness and finding natural a woman who has not covered hers. This is his opinion on a disputed matter. (needs_source: the wording of the passage is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:34:30}
- He says that were he given a licence for a satellite channel he would show what pleases the Prophet and leave what does not, and tells that villagers in the 1960s covered their eyes at scenes of tennis and synchronised swimming. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:36:30}
- He says we follow the West in fashion but not in seriousness, honesty, truthfulness, paying taxes, invention, freedom and the rights of the governed, and tells that Umar waded through a pool of mud on entering Jerusalem and said we were humble and God honoured us by Islam. (needs_source: the story of Umar has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:37:30}
- He imagines America cutting the undersea internet cables from the Muslims and says the community must be a producer and not a consumer, and says Ibn al-Qayyim said that if non-Muslims excel in something and no Muslim devotes himself to it, all Muslims are guilty. (needs_source: the attribution to Ibn al-Qayyim has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:39:00}
- He advises anyone whose breast is tight to make ablution, pray two cycles and seek forgiveness, and relates by sense that whoever asks forgiveness much, God gives him relief from every worry and a way out from every distress. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:39:30}
- He says a Muslim asks God seventeen times a day to guide him on the straight path, that the shortest road between two points is a straight line, and cites following the path and leaving other ways and what God enjoined on the prophets, to establish the religion and not divide in it. See [[passage/quran-1-6|al-Fatiha, verse 6]], [[passage/quran-6-153|al-An'am, verse 153]] and [[passage/quran-42-13|al-Shura, verse 13]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:40:30}
- He gives two measures of guidance: first that a believer has no choice once God and His Messenger decide a matter, and he tells that the Children of Israel said they heard; second that they do not believe until they make the Prophet judge in their disputes and then find no discomfort in what he decided and submit. See [[passage/quran-33-36|al-Ahzab, verse 36]] and [[passage/quran-4-65|al-Nisa, verse 65]]. (needs_source: what the Children of Israel said is garbled in the transcript) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:42:00}
- He says the sincere Muslim is the one who knows God's right and fulfils it, and God then guides him to the even path. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep07 00:42:00}

## Episode 8: beautiful patience
- He pauses at God's command to be patient with His decree because the Prophet is under His eyes, says patience is not easy and is a practical way and not a word to be said, and notes that the Qur'an describes patience, forgiving and withdrawing as beautiful. See [[passage/quran-52-48|al-Tur, verse 48]], [[passage/quran-12-18|Yusuf, verse 18]], [[passage/quran-15-85|al-Hijr, verse 85]] and [[passage/quran-73-10|al-Muzzammil, verse 10]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:03:00}
- He holds that patience is a positive stance because it is half of faith, and says faith is half patience and half gratitude. (needs_source: the saying is unattributed) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:03:30}
- He explains beautiful forgiving as forgiving without reproach, as between a father and his son, and beautiful withdrawal as lessening visits to relatives who backbite and plunge into people's honour, seeing them on occasions without cutting them off. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:05:30}
- He says the address to the Prophet in "be patient with your Lord's decree" is one the community is honoured to be addressed by, that whoever is patient with his Lord's decree enters His company and care, and contrasts God's words to Moses, "that you be brought up under My eye", with His words to the Prophet, "you are under Our eyes". See [[passage/quran-20-39|Ta Ha, verse 39]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:06:30}
- He criticises calling some illnesses incurable and malignant because physicians have not yet reached their cure, as they did for tuberculosis and malaria, and relates by sense that the Prophet said God sent down no disease without a cure except old age. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:08:30}
- He says the heart of a mother whose child has a fever is like the heart of Moses' mother, empty save for fear for him, then lists the chain of people on whom his treatment depends: the doctor and his family, nurses, paper mills, the pharmacist and packaging factories, and says God sets people to serve one another. See [[passage/quran-28-10|al-Qasas, verse 10]], [[passage/quran-2-251|al-Baqara, verse 251]] and [[passage/quran-22-40|al-Hajj, verse 40]]. (needs_source: part of the passage is a repeated loop) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:11:30}
- He says every trade is needed by the city and praises sweepers and drain workers, says he would like to kiss their hands, and that they are more honourable than someone in fine clothes who eats the forbidden. This is his opinion. (needs_source: part of the passage is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:14:00}
- He says that when a believer is struck by calamity he asks who is trying him and calms down, since God is kinder to him than his father and mother, and that some trials are among the gentle favours, as when God keeps a man from a gathering of disobedience by an accident, a death or a power cut. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:16:30}
- He says God made the night a covering and the day for livelihood, criticises staying up late, and states that scholars say a person who does not sleep before midnight has his melatonin disrupted and becomes depressed. (needs_source: the scientific claim has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:17:30}
- He says he is only a reminder and that the preacher advises and does not dominate, and cites God's command to pardon and turn away from the ignorant. See [[passage/quran-88-21|al-Ghashiya, verse 21]] and [[passage/quran-7-199|al-A'raf, verse 199]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:18:30}
- He distinguishes the one who gives thanks in ease from the one who is thankful in affliction as well, and says such a person says "we belong to God and to Him we return". (needs_source: the linguistic distinction has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:19:30}
- He mentions the saying that you may dislike something and it is good for you, and criticises families that count only medicine and engineering as prestigious colleges and look down on arts, law, media and decoration. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-2-216|al-Baqara, verse 216]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:20:30}
- He says the Arab used to look down on work, that the word for profession comes from degradation, and that Islam came to raise the standing of work, and relates by sense that the Prophet said God have mercy on my brother David who ate from the work of his hand. (needs_source: the derivation of the word and the report are without a source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:21:30}
- He mentions that David and Solomon, despite the kingdom they were given, were told to work in thanks, and that Umar would ask a man's trade and, if he had none, the man fell in his esteem. See [[passage/quran-34-10|Saba, verse 10]] and [[passage/quran-34-11|Saba, verse 11]]. (needs_source: the report of Umar has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:22:30}
- He calls for using older experienced preachers as a football team uses an expert coach, for not killing creativity, and says every person who works is respected and one who does not is a burden. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:24:30}
- He advises anyone struck by calamity to look at the afflicted and visit hospitals, and relates by sense that whoever wakes secure in his household, healthy in body and with his day's food, it is as if the whole world were gathered for him. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:26:30}
- He mentions people in countries of the region who go out unsafe for themselves, and recalls the blessing of sitting, moving and thinking. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:27:30}
- He tells that a man's field yielded less than usual and he grieved, and God told Moses to tell his neighbour to take supper to his neighbour the widow and her orphans, and she said she does not carry tomorrow's worry because the morning's provision comes from the Provider; he cites that provision is in heaven. See [[passage/quran-51-22|al-Dhariyat, verse 22]]. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:30:00}
- He tells of a man aged 102 whom doctors told forty-two years ago he would die of cancer within months, who lived on while they died. (needs_source: the tale has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:31:30}
- He warns against opening the black box of one's life, brooding on memories and complaining of relatives' wrongs. This is his opinion. (needs_source: part of the passage is a repeated loop) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:32:30}
- He relates by sense that when something distressed the Prophet he turned to prayer and said give us rest by it, Bilal, and says this world is treated with religion and not with the world. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:34:00}
- He tells that a man sat in the mosque from worries and debts and the Prophet taught him a prayer for refuge from worry, grief, incapacity, laziness, cowardice, miserliness, the weight of debt and the domination of men, and he said it and God removed his worry and paid his debt; he urges saying it with certainty. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:34:30}
- He cites God's promise to answer whoever calls on Him and says that being given the door of supplication means God wants to give. See [[passage/quran-40-60|Ghafir, verse 60]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:35:30}
- He says everything decreed comes to be, that the finest person is the patient one who complains to no one, and recites lines on patience that he attributes to Ali. (needs_source: the attribution of the lines to Ali has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:37:30}
- He says whoever is sad should turn to Surat Yusuf, blessings on the Prophet, ablution, prayer and remembrance of God. See [[passage/quran-29-45|al-Ankabut, verse 45]]. (needs_source: his statement on the effect of Surat Yusuf has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep08 00:38:30}

## Episode 9: feeding the hungry, caring for the orphan and giving advice
- He pauses at the verse "they feed, for love of Him, the poor, the orphan and the captive", counts feeding, spreading peace and praying at night while people sleep among good deeds, and says a believer is the brother of a believer and does not wrong or forsake him. See [[passage/quran-76-8|al-Insan, verse 8]], [[passage/quran-76-9|al-Insan, verse 9]], [[passage/quran-76-10|al-Insan, verse 10]] and [[passage/quran-49-10|al-Hujurat, verse 10]]. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:01:30}
- He passes on from the Hanbalis that if a Muslim dies of hunger every capable person around him is guilty and owes the blood-money, and says all who can are guilty when a hungry Muslim anywhere perishes, and criticises the food waste thrown away in Ramadan. (needs_source: the attribution to the Hanbalis has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:03:00}
- He relates by sense the Prophet's praise of the Ash'aris who, when their provisions ran low, put what they had in one vessel and divided it equally, and that he said they are from me and I am from them. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:04:00}
- He says God put the poor man's sustenance in the surplus of the rich man's wealth, so no poor man goes hungry except through a rich man's glut. (needs_source: the saying is unattributed) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:05:00}
- He says those living in camps after being driven from their lands are a trust on our necks, that we should feed them and get aid to them by any means, and count it an obligation on ourselves. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:06:00}
- He tells that when anyone knocked at the door of al-Hasan al-Basri he came out with his purse in his right hand and his sword in his left, saying if it is a guest this is my money and if an enemy this is my sword. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:06:30}
- He tells that Umar went out at night with Abd al-Rahman ibn Awf and they heard a woman and children crying and a pot of water boiling to quiet them so they would sleep, and she said she had nothing to feed them and called on Umar for not knowing; Umar took flour and ghee from the treasury and carried the sack himself, asking Ibn Awf whether he would bear his burden on the Day of Resurrection, cooked for them and blew on the fire until smoke came from his beard, and stayed until he saw them laughing. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:12:00}
- He says he advises mothers not to blow on their children's food because of germs, then says Umar had the light of faith. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:11:00}
- He tells that Umar rubbed a date on the palate of al-Hasan al-Basri so that he spoke only wisdom, and that a man was healed when Umar recited the Fatiha and laid a hand on the sore place but when Umar died the illness came back and another's recitation did not help, and says the pure hand that gives alms is not like others. (needs_source: the story has no chain and the passage is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:11:30}
- He tells that the woman came next morning and found the man who had made her children laugh was the Commander of the Faithful, who asked what she would sell her grievance for and had the purchase of the grievance witnessed by Abd al-Rahman ibn Awf, Ammar ibn Yasir and Ali, and said put it in my shroud. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:17:30}
- He mentions that God will have a servant stand on the Day of Resurrection and unroll ninety-nine scrolls each as far as the eye can see, and calls on us to return to the hereafter. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:13:30}
- He relates by sense that on the Day of Resurrection God will take retribution for the hornless sheep from the horned one, and that whoever takes a span of land wrongfully will be encircled with it from seven earths. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:14:00}
- He says whoever wants God to lift injustice from him should lift injustice from his wife, his children, his family and his employees, and that whoever's power tempts him to injustice should remember God's power over him. This is his opinion. (needs_source: part of the passage is a repeated loop) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:15:00}
- He passes on advice to the sense of fit out the ship because the sea is deep, take much provision because the journey is long and make your intention sincere because the Examiner sees, and says God sees a man's tear as he lies beside his wife. (needs_source: the advice is unattributed) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:15:30}
- He urges complaining to God and not to people, citing Jacob's words, and mentions a line of al-Mutanabbi that complaint to a kinsman consoles, then says it gives nothing. See [[passage/quran-12-86|Yusuf, verse 86]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:16:30}
- He relates by sense that authority is disgrace and regret on the Day of Resurrection except for one who takes it rightly, that whoever seeks it is left to it, and says the head has many headaches. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:18:30}
- He says he has no enmity with anyone and that his only enemy is Satan, advises every official, from president and minister to watchman, to fear God in what he has been entrusted with, and quotes a line to the sense that advice is the dearest thing sold or given. (needs_source: the line is unattributed) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:19:30}
- He says a rich man's feeding of the poor is a duty imposed by the laws of heaven and not a social custom because believers are brothers, and relates by sense that charity never reduced a servant's wealth. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:21:00}
- He explains the difference between sight and insight with the example of one who gives ten of a hundred: sight sees ninety, insight sees a hundred and ninety because ten is tenfold with God; he relates by sense that God takes charity in His right hand and rears it as one of you rears his foal, and that the Prophet said protect yourselves from the Fire even with half a date. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:22:30}
- He says do not belittle the little, that deprivation is less, and tells that Marie Antoinette told the hungry people to eat cake if they had no bread. (needs_source: the attribution of the remark has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:23:00}
- He calls for the left hand not to know what the right spends, and tells that Ali Zayn al-Abidin carried sacks of flour by night to the poor of Medina and was known for it only after his death, when marks of the ropes showed on his shoulders. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:24:30}
- He says the captive in the verse is a non-Muslim taken in wars and has a right to charity, and defines the poor man as one whom poverty has made still so that he does not ask and whom the ignorant take for rich from his self-restraint, such as an employee whose pay does not last the month. (needs_source: his interpretation of the captive and his definition are his own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:25:30}
- He urges bringing a restrained poor person his month's provisions and not only in Ramadan, and relates by sense that the people of Paradise are a just ruler, a merciful tender-hearted man and a chaste man with dependants. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:27:00}
- He speaks of the orphan and urges kindness to him, relates by sense that whoever strokes an orphan's head has a reward for every hair and that the best house is one where an orphan is honoured, and urges taking an orphan to the iftar table, sponsoring him, visiting him and giving him new clothes for Eid. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:30:00}
- He relates by sense that a woman captive with her infant divided the dates between him and herself and the Prophet said God is more merciful to His believing servant than this woman to her child, and says have mercy on those on earth. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:31:00}
- He tells that Moses asked Qarun for zakat and he denied that wealth is God's and said he had been given it because of knowledge he had, and God made the earth swallow him; he says three thousand billionaires in America own as much as the national product of forty-nine poor countries. (needs_source: the story and the statistic have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:32:30}
- He says Dhul-Qarnayn, when the people asked for a dam, took raw materials from them and built with them, and says the West stands beside the failing until he succeeds while we stand in front of the successful until he fails. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-18-94|al-Kahf, verse 94]], [[passage/quran-18-95|al-Kahf, verse 95]] and [[passage/quran-18-96|al-Kahf, verse 96]]. (needs_source: part of the passage is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:33:30}
- He says believers advise one another and advice in public is disgrace, that advice should be kind and timely, relates by sense that religion is sincere advice to God, His Messenger, the leaders of the Muslims and their common people, and calls feigning not to notice a Sunna. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:35:30}
- He tells that Aisha used to perfume a dirham before giving it and place it somewhere high and not in the poor man's hand, and says the One who receives is God. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:37:30}
- He tells that a poor man came to the Prophet to beg and the Prophet sold his bowl and rug for two dirhams, bought food for his family and an axe and told him to gather firewood for fifteen days; he relates by sense that begging is lawful only for severe poverty, a crushing debt or a painful blood-price, and says Islam gives the poor man his need and not a career. (needs_source: the story and the report are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep09 00:39:00}

## Episode 10: whoever wants the fleeting world and whoever wants the hereafter
- He pauses at the verses that separate whoever wants the fleeting world from whoever wants the hereafter and strives for it while a believer, and says people are of two kinds, those who want this world only and those who want the hereafter and this world with it; he says the early generations understood the world as the farm of the hereafter and built it by the logic of the hereafter, so they did not backbite, steal or take bribes. See [[passage/quran-17-18|al-Isra, verse 18]], [[passage/quran-17-19|al-Isra, verse 19]] and [[passage/quran-17-20|al-Isra, verse 20]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:02:30}
- He tells that Talha asked the Prophet how, on the Day of Resurrection, he would know he was the one called among so many named Talha, and the Prophet told him that when Talha ibn Ubaydallah is called none will rise but you. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:03:30}
- He mentions that God fastened each person's deeds to his neck and will bring out for him a book to read on the Day of Resurrection. See [[passage/quran-17-13|al-Isra, verse 13]] and [[passage/quran-17-14|al-Isra, verse 14]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:04:00}
- He tells that Einstein forgot his glasses in a restaurant and asked the waiter to read him the menu, and the waiter said he was illiterate like him, and likens this to a person with religious illiteracy who does not care about the lawful and forbidden. (needs_source: the anecdote has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:05:00}
- He likens the lover of this world to one who drinks seawater and is only made thirstier, and relates by sense that if the son of Adam had a valley of gold he would wish for two and nothing fills his belly but earth. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:06:00}
- He says forty-nine per cent of the world's poor are Muslims and that the fifth owed on buried treasure and mineral wealth, which he applies to the oil of four Muslim states, came to about fifty-three billion dollars in a year and would, if directed to Somalia, southern Kenya and Ghana, enrich the needy. (needs_source: the figures and the application of this fifth to oil have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:08:30}
- He warns of three things that cause depression: filling one's mind with poverty, and living above one's means by borrowing from a bank to buy a car beyond one's pay, so he advises living within one's income; he relates by sense the prohibition on a man burdening himself beyond his capacity and cites the proverb stretch your legs to the length of your blanket. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:12:00}
- He tells businessmen and ordinary citizens alike not to enter into servitude to a bank. This is his opinion. (needs_source: part of the passage is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:11:00}
- He makes the second point not dwelling on one who wronged you so that your heart does not harden, and describes a person who thinks everyone in his workplace hates him and deals with them on that basis. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:13:00}
- He makes the third point not thinking of staying long in this world, and explains by arithmetic that any number divided by infinity is zero, so the world compared with the hereafter, and relates by sense that if the world weighed a gnat's wing with God He would not give an unbeliever a drink from it. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:14:30}
- He says three and a half billion of seven billion do not believe God exists and God still provides for them, since He is merciful and forbearing and does not hasten punishment. (needs_source: the figure has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:15:00}
- He tells that God uncovered the dominion for Abraham, who saw a man beating an orphan and another robbing a widow and prayed against them, and God said leave Me and My servants, whoever repents I am his lover and whoever does not I am his physician. See [[passage/quran-6-75|al-An'am, verse 75]]. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:16:30}
- He relates by sense that a man asked the Prophet how often to forgive his servant and he said seventy times a day, and says the point is abundance and a wide breast. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:17:30}
- He gathers the three rules, not to think of poverty, of those who wronged you or of lasting in the world, and says the angel of death has nothing to do with age or illness, giving the example of a footballer who collapsed on the pitch and a child who dies. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:19:30}
- He says whoever wants the world is given it, that a trust is accounted for, and that some people ask God for wealth, grow rich and then drop out of the lessons of knowledge. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:20:30}
- He says the world should be a passage and a bridge and not a home, and relates by sense that the Prophet left the community on a white path whose night is like its day and from which only the lost strays. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:21:30}
- He likens neglect of a hardened heart to the breathlessness after climbing stairs that sends one to the doctor, and asks whether the last time was when you wept in prayer, rose in the night or finished the Qur'an. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:23:30}
- He recommends reading at least a part of the Qur'an each day, says the reward is guaranteed whether one understands or not, and tells that someone was asked which deed is best and said reading the Qur'an with understanding and without. (needs_source: the report has no chain and the name of the one asked is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:24:30}
- He relates by sense the divine hadith that the servant keeps drawing near by extra devotions until God loves him and becomes his hearing, and contrasts people's patience with a two-hour match with their impatience with the Friday sermon. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:25:30}
- He relates by sense that when the imam mounts the pulpit the angels fold the registers and sit to listen, and quotes a saying to the sense that the covenant between us and the idolaters is prayer, so whoever abandons it has disbelieved, and says disbelief has kinds. (needs_source: both reports are by sense and without a chain, and his account of the kinds of disbelief is his own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:26:00}
- He says the heart is revived by prayer and remembrance as the earth is by rain, and that a heart that has risen to God is not eavesdropped on by devils, who are struck by a watching flame when they approach heaven. See [[passage/quran-72-8|al-Jinn, verse 8]] and [[passage/quran-72-9|al-Jinn, verse 9]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:27:30}
- He says acts of worship are varied lest they weary, and that everything, food, sleep and marriage, can be for God if done to seek His pleasure. See [[passage/quran-6-162|al-An'am, verse 162]] and [[passage/quran-6-163|al-An'am, verse 163]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:29:00}
- He says many people speak of dying in God's way and that he wants scholars and preachers to speak of living in God's way, with outstanding schools, universities, doctors, and honest journalists and media. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the context of a contemporary conflict is dropped from the passage) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:30:00}
- He passes on from Abu Dharr that in 36 AH he said the people had been a rose without thorns and had become thorns without a rose. (needs_source: the ascription to Abu Dharr and its dating have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:30:30}
- He cites a saying he ascribes to Abu Dharr that men are of four kinds: one who knows and knows he knows, follow him; one who knows and does not know it, a humble scholar; one who does not know and knows it, teach him; and one who does not know and does not know it, a devil to flee from; he wishes to hear Arabs say "God knows" and quotes two lines in the voice of the sage's donkey. (needs_source: the ascription of the four kinds to Abu Dharr and the two lines have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:33:00}
- He distinguishes life from subsistence, saying the good life is for believers and whoever turns from remembering God has a narrow subsistence. See [[passage/quran-16-97|al-Nahl, verse 97]], [[passage/quran-20-124|Ta Ha, verse 124]], [[passage/quran-20-125|Ta Ha, verse 125]] and [[passage/quran-20-126|Ta Ha, verse 126]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:34:30}
- He says God created love of good in the servant and attributed the good to him, that God guides whom He wills meaning whoever wills guidance, and gives the examples of Abu Bakr and Abu Jahl, to both of whom the message was offered and one believed while the other shut the door, and of a student who studies and passes against one who does not and fails, citing that whoever wills let him believe. This is his explanation. See [[passage/quran-18-29|al-Kahf, verse 29]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:37:30}
- He passes on from Muhammad Iqbal that the weak believer makes excuses with fate and the strong believer is God's fate, and says a lazy boy cannot excuse failing by decree. (needs_source: the ascription to Iqbal has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:38:30}
- He relates by sense that Umar forbade anyone to lie on his back saying "O God, provide for me" when the sky does not rain gold or silver, and tells of a man who saw a healthy crow feeding a crippled one and sat in the mosque waiting for provision, so Umar struck him with the whip and told him be the healthy crow. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:40:00}
- He says good is not limited to building a mosque but includes building a hospital, a medicine factory and sponsoring a gifted student's education, and that the circle of good widens. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:41:00}
- He tells that when a sparrow pecked twice at the sea Khidr told Moses that his knowledge and Moses' knowledge compared with God's was like what the sparrow took from the sea. See [[passage/quran-18-65|al-Kahf, verse 65]]. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep10 00:42:00}

## Episode 11: sleep, the spirit and the trust of work
- He recalls the programme's method of stopping each day at a verse to see what a Muslim takes from it, says the world is in turmoil and security lies in returning to the Creator who knows what mends and what spoils a person, and likens this to the baby who calms only in his mother's arms because he got used to her heartbeat. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:02:30}
- He says God drew two roads for a person, the road of the lawful and happiness and the road of the forbidden and misery, that those on the right road are few, and that swimming against the current is the hardest swimming, since a husband, wife or children may be a stumbling block to one's aspiration in faith. See [[passage/quran-64-14|al-Taghabun, verse 14]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:04:30}
- He tells that a scholar, whose name is garbled in the transcript, was asked at his death for a piece of wisdom and said work for God as much as you need Him, work for this world as much as your stay in it, work for the hereafter as much as your stay there and work for the Fire as much as your patience with it. (needs_source: the wisdom is ascribed to a scholar whose name is garbled and has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:05:30}
- He explains that a person cannot do without God for the blink of an eye, otherwise he becomes poor in faith and in means and blessing is removed from his family and work, and cites God making forget those who forgot Him. See [[passage/quran-59-19|al-Hashr, verse 19]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:06:30}
- He likens this world to a finger dipped in the sea, repeats that the years of a life divided by infinity equal zero, and mentions that the guilty will swear on the Day of Resurrection that they stayed only an hour and that they stayed a day or part of a day. This is his explanation. See [[passage/quran-30-55|al-Rum, verse 55]], [[passage/quran-23-112|al-Mu'minun, verse 112]] and [[passage/quran-23-113|al-Mu'minun, verse 113]]. (needs_source: the finger-in-the-sea likeness is given by sense) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:09:00}
- He describes how fast the years pass through a father at his son's graduation, his daughter's wedding and then his grandchild, and says one travelling two days takes a small bag but one who travels without return needs bags of deeds. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:11:00}
- He tells that Umar would hold his finger near the fire and say do you have patience for this, son of al-Khattab. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:11:30}
- He says happiness is from within and not from outside, that the envious are not happy even if they own everything, that a rich man who clings to his money does not give of it, and criticises the rich who do not pay the zakat on their wealth and look for a jurist to reckon it lower. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:13:00}
- He tells of a man who asked an Arab jurist to compute his zakat and it came to seven million dollars, so he asked another jurist, who computed two million. (needs_source: the story has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:14:00}
- He tells that when the verse of the goodly loan came down Abu al-Dahdah asked the Prophet whether our Lord was borrowing from us, and lent a garden he owned and no other, and told his wife Umm al-Dahdah, who came out with her children and said the sale was profitable. See [[passage/quran-2-245|al-Baqara, verse 245]]. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:15:30}
- He says sleep is among the things that puzzled scholars, that God takes souls at death and those that have not died in their sleep, that God made night a garment and day for livelihood, and that some of the community reversed life, staying up at night and sleeping by day, and so find no ease of breast. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-39-42|al-Zumar, verse 42]], [[passage/quran-78-10|al-Naba, verse 10]] and [[passage/quran-78-11|al-Naba, verse 11]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:17:00}
- He says the spirit is subtle and made of light and we know only a little of it, that a sleeper's spirit leaves his body so that he may see loved ones who have died, that the body veils the spirit like a room with walls but in sleep the body becomes lighter, and that God keeps the spirit of one He decrees to die and releases the other to a set term. See [[passage/quran-17-85|al-Isra, verse 85]]. (needs_source: his description of what happens to the spirit in sleep has no source and one stretch is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:21:00}
- He tells that he sleeps uncovered and wakes to find himself covered, says scholars say the brain signals the hand but asks who gave the brain the signal, and returns to the Creator's power. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:22:00}
- He says there are two kinds of sleep, biological sleep and a civilisational sleep that the community has slept for centuries because it understood religion as acts of worship and rituals alone without their fruit. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:23:30}
- He says the spirit in sleep is subtler and brighter so matters are uncovered to it and visions come, that some see nightmares because the mind is occupied, quotes a line to the sense let the decrees run their course and do not sleep but free of care, and says God moves people from state to state in the blink of an eye. (needs_source: the line is unattributed) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:25:30}
- He tells a woman who thinks the marriage train has passed her that it is a provision whose time has not come, that one must supplicate, and that he begins his sleep with the sleep remembrances and asks God to enable him to pray the dawn prayer. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:26:30}
- He says provision is distributed at the dawn prayer and that whoever prays dawn after sunrise suffers the removal of blessing from provision, time and children, and relates by sense that Satan urinates in the ear of one who sleeps through prayer. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:27:30}
- He says God is shy to return empty the hands of a servant who raises them, relates by sense that the best deed is prayer at its time, criticises those who fill the mosques in Ramadan and then abandon them, and says he wants to worship the Lord of Ramadan and not Ramadan. (needs_source: both reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:31:00}
- He reckons that one who lives sixty years sleeps twenty and works about twenty, leaving only a few years, calls on a person to review his accounts, and says night prayer and the dawn prayer are an honour from God to the servant, as the state honours one who has served his country. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:34:00}
- He says the chief devils are bound in Ramadan and some nightmares come from human devils, and criticises Ramadan serials, riddle shows and cooking programmes that made Ramadan a matter of consumption. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:36:00}
- He says he does not belittle cooks, mentions that God said eat of the good things We have provided, and says the right place for food is the home and that children are taking hormones, telling that a fast-food chain funded genetic research for chickens without feathers. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-2-172|al-Baqara, verse 172]]. (needs_source: the story of the featherless chickens has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:40:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:41:00}
- He says everything is a trust, from the gathering to the screen, the wife, upbringing, the car, the home and the country, that trust requires truthfulness in small and great, and that sweeping and sewage work are noble trades, a sweeper's hand being cleaner than the hand of one who takes bribes. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-4-58|al-Nisa, verse 58]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:41:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:41:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:42:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:42:30}
- He closes by saying sleep needs a review of our accounts, that we should sleep in clothes bought with lawful money on a bed bought with lawful money so as to see good visions. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:42:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep11 00:43:00}

## Episode 12: tightness of the chest and the jurisprudence of calamity
- He pauses at the last verses of Surat al-Hijr on the Prophet's chest being tight at what they say and the command to glorify, prostrate and worship his Lord until certainty comes, and says a person may feel tightness of chest with no apparent cause and should look for the causes of ease. See [[passage/quran-15-97|al-Hijr, verse 97]], [[passage/quran-15-98|al-Hijr, verse 98]] and [[passage/quran-15-99|al-Hijr, verse 99]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:02:30}
- He says trial has its etiquette, and the first thing the afflicted does is to await the reward; he mentions what is befalling Muslims in every part of the earth, and relates by sense that the nations will call one another against the Muslims as diners call to a bowl, that they will be many but scum like the scum of a flood, and that God will remove awe from their enemies' hearts and cast weakness into theirs. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:05:00}
- He says Khosrow trembled at the news of Umar and that Umar's whip was more feared than al-Hajjaj's sword, and contrasts the present weakness of the Muslims before those who take their wealth and influence their education and media. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the report of Khosrow has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:05:00}
- He advises anyone struck by calamity not to add the calamity of panic to it and to look at the afflicted, and calls for visiting hospitals and the bone, heart, liver, blood and children's wards. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:07:30}
- He advises that the afflicted say praise be to God that it is not in religion, and says the true calamity is a son leaving prayer, a girl taking off the headscarf or a husband taking drugs, and that a servant is owned by God and should submit his affair to Him. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:09:00}
- He calls on people to look at the side of blessings in a calamity and at the trials of societies from disease, disorder and earthquakes, and tells of a man who sold his sandals and entered the mosque barefoot and sad, saw a man with both feet cut off and thanked God. (needs_source: the tale has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep12 00:10:30}
