The subject is connected to [[person/sulayman|Solomon]]. (needs_source: Solomon's age and the details of his ruling are not in the verse and have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:29:30}
- He tells of a young man whose home internet was cut for three days and who discovered his parents and siblings were good people, and says the West used the internet for science and we use it to sever family ties. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the story has no chain and names no one) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:25:30}
- He says David, when he read the Psalms, was echoed by the mountains and birds, that the water stopped flowing at his beautiful voice, and relates by sense that the Prophet heard [[person/abu-musa-al-ashari|Abu Musa]] reciting and said he had been given a flute of the family of David, to which Abu Musa answered that had he known he was listening he would have adorned it. The subject is connected to [[person/dawud|David]]. See [[passage/quran-34-10|Saba, verse 10]]. (needs_source: the water stopping has no chain, and the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:31:00}
- He quotes one of the righteous praying that God would not punish him in a scene before the Prophet because he is ashamed to claim love for him and not follow his Sunna, and relates that [[person/abu-bakr|Abu Bakr]] did not strip his clothes even alone because the angels were with him. (needs_source: the prayer is credited to an unnamed righteous man, and the account of Abu Bakr has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep22 00:32:00}

## Episode 23: how Satan is resisted from every side
- He pauses at the verses of Surat al-A'raf in which Iblis is granted respite until the day they are raised, and says he will sit in wait on the straight path for the children of Adam and come at them from before, behind, right and left. He says Satan surrounds a person with whispering and with his troops from mankind and jinn, and that the devils return to their tasks after Ramadan. See [[passage/quran-7-14|al-A'raf, verse 14]], [[passage/quran-7-15|al-A'raf, verse 15]], [[passage/quran-7-16|al-A'raf, verse 16]] and [[passage/quran-7-17|al-A'raf, verse 17]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:02:30}
- He says the first thing by which Satan is overcome is persistence in remembrance, in obedience to God's command to remember Him often, and he widens the meaning of remembrance by saying every limb has its own. See [[passage/quran-33-41|al-Ahzab, verse 41]], [[passage/quran-33-42|al-Ahzab, verse 42]] and [[passage/quran-33-43|al-Ahzab, verse 43]]. The subject is connected to [[concept/dhikr|remembrance]]. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:03:30}
- He lists the remembrance of each limb: the eye, by looking at the dominion of heaven and earth, at the remains of those who perished and at people with mercy and not with disdain; the ear, by not hearing obscenity and by hearing good words and reconciliation; the hand, by charity, stroking an orphan's head, writing good things and helping people; the foot, by walking to mosques and to mending quarrels; the heart, by certainty and true reliance and freedom from rancour, spite, envy, pride and conceit. See [[passage/quran-14-45|Ibrahim, verse 45]] and [[passage/quran-14-46|Ibrahim, verse 46]]. This is his division. (needs_source: the division is his own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:08:30}
- He advises spouses not to mock each other's taste, since taste includes the choice of the partner, and calls it shameful. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:07:00}
- He likens the heart to a palace with guards, so that one who surrounds it with the light of remembrance finds Satan does not approach, as a thief does not approach a king's palace, and says seeking refuge in God is standing beside God, since Satan sees a person and is not seen. See [[passage/quran-23-97|al-Mu'minun, verse 97]] and [[passage/quran-23-98|al-Mu'minun, verse 98]]. The subject is connected to [[concept/taawwudh|seeking refuge]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:10:00}
- He relates by sense the Prophet's counsel to [[person/abdullah-ibn-abbas|Ibn Abbas]] when he was a boy: guard God and He will guard you, when you ask ask God and when you seek help seek help from God, and if the whole community gathered to benefit you they could benefit you only with what God had written for you, the pens are lifted and the pages dry. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:11:00}
- He criticises amulets, blue beads, horseshoes and hanging chains, relates by sense that whoever hangs an amulet, God will not complete it for him, and that [[person/abdullah-ibn-masud|Ibn Masud]] saw an amulet around his wife's neck, pulled it off and said the family of Ibn Masud had no need of polytheism. This is his opinion. The subject is connected to [[concept/tiyara|omens]]. (needs_source: the report and the account are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:12:30}
- He says the devils of mankind tempt people through screens and markets, that Satan does not sleep, and relates that [[person/al-hasan-al-basri|al-Hasan al-Basri]] was asked whether Satan sleeps and said that if he slept we would rest, and that God did not decree sleep for him except at the first blast of the trumpet. (needs_source: the report on al-Hasan al-Basri and the saying on the trumpet have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:18:30}
- He relates by sense that Iblis gathers his troops each day and asks which of them misled most, one saying he misled a certain person until he left the night prayer, which does not please Iblis, another saying he set two brothers at odds and divorced a man's wife, and Iblis puts the crown on his head and tells him he is the one, and says those who divide the beloved are among the worst of servants before God. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:20:00}
- He lists remedies against Satan: persistence in remembrance, seeking refuge, following the Book and the Sunna, renewing repentance and moderation, saying Satan wants to take a person to the extremes so that he becomes excessive. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-2-143|al-Baqara, verse 143]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:21:00}
- He says generosity lies between waste and miserliness, relates by sense that the Prophet was more generous than the blowing wind in Ramadan, and that al-Aqra ibn Habis, so written in the transcript, returned to his people after the Prophet gave him hundreds of camels and sheep at Hunayn and said Muhammad gives like one who does not fear poverty. He adds that the wasteful are the brothers of the devils and that the Prophet forbade excess and said the hair-splitters are ruined. The subject is connected to [[concept/karam|generosity]]. See [[passage/quran-17-27|al-Isra, verse 27]] and [[passage/quran-4-171|al-Nisa, verse 171]]. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain, and the name and numbers stand as in the machine transcript) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:23:30}
- He says honour lies between pride and abasement and cites verses he credits to [[person/amr-ibn-kulthum|Amr ibn Kulthum]] boasting that the mighty fall prostrate before their infant. He quotes a well-known saying about heads that are ripe for the cutting and asks how many tyrants have passed through time, says whoever raises iron against his brother, God's protection is withdrawn from him, forbids saying one is a servant under orders, and says obedience is in what is right and that God commanded that parents not be obeyed in polytheism. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-63-8|al-Munafiqun, verse 8]] and [[passage/quran-31-15|Luqman, verse 15]]. (needs_source: the attribution of the verses, the well-known saying and the report are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:26:30}
- He says courage lies between recklessness and cowardice, that Islam commands neither recklessness nor licence, and mentions the Arab woman who split her garment at the side and was told by God not to stamp her feet to show her hidden adornment, saying Islam is against shameful mixing and against isolation and wants familiarity within the limits of the law. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-2-195|al-Baqara, verse 195]] and [[passage/quran-24-31|al-Nur, verse 31]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:29:00}
- He relates by sense that the palm trunk the Prophet used to preach from moaned like a child when the Ansari made him a pulpit, so he embraced it and had it buried in the mosque roof, that he said Uhud is a mountain that loves us and we love it, and that the nearest to him in seat are the best in character, those easy to approach who befriend and are befriended. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:31:00}
- He cites verses by Ahmad Shawqi describing the Prophet's mercy and faithfulness, relates by sense that the Prophet was asked who is the most noble of people and said Joseph, and says Umar was strong against truth before Islam and that he said in his sermon that he had severity and God should make it against the people of falsehood. (needs_source: the attribution of the verses, the report and the account of Umar have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:32:00}
- He says the scales have turned over because we have left the devils loose, that Iblis refused to bow by saying I am better than he, that the swollen self is in each of us, and that Satan does not wait at the door of places of entertainment for those are already his troops, but comes to the upright to make them show off, belittle charity and delay prayer. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:35:00}
- He relates by sense that when the Prophet shook a person's hand he laid his other hand over it and did not withdraw until the other did, that his hand seemed to come from a perfumer's basket, and advises perfume, cleanliness, fitting clothes when meeting people and not wearing nightclothes or dirty socks in mosques. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:37:00}
- He says dress is a people's custom and what suits one country may not suit another, and tells that [[person/muawiya-ibn-abi-sufyan|Muawiya]] met Umar at the conquest of Jerusalem in splendid dress which did not please Umar, who then told him he neither commanded nor forbade him. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:37:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:40:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep23 00:40:30}

## Episode 24: seeking refuge from the whisperings of the devils, and what ruins the body, gives joy, breeds hatred and brings provision
- He pauses at the verse of Surat al-Mu'minun on seeking refuge in the Lord from the whisperings of the devils and from their presence, and explains the word Satan as coming from distance, so that Satan takes a person away from good and brings him near evil, from remembrance to heedlessness, from contentment to discontent, from joy to sorrow. See [[passage/quran-23-97|al-Mu'minun, verse 97]], [[passage/quran-23-98|al-Mu'minun, verse 98]] and [[passage/quran-6-153|al-An'am, verse 153]]. (needs_source: the derivation of the word has no attribution) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:02:30}
- He mentions that God breathed into Adam of His spirit and told the angels to bow to him, and credits [[person/al-hasan-al-basri|al-Hasan al-Basri]] with saying of Satan that if obeyed he does not benefit and if disobeyed he does not harm, since benefit and harm are in God's hand. See [[passage/quran-15-29|al-Hijr, verse 29]]. (needs_source: the saying is credited to al-Hasan al-Basri with no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:03:30}
- He lists four things that ruin the body: worry, grief, too much staying up and too much hunger or eating the unlawful. He relates by sense the prayer for refuge from worry and grief, cites lines on turning away from worries and leaving matters to destiny, says Satan wants a person to think of the problem and not the solution, that youths in Ramadan sleep by day and stay up at night, and that most of the Muslims' victories came in Ramadan. The subject is connected to [[concept/huzn|grief]]. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain, the lines are unattributed, and the claim on victories has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:07:00}
- He takes up usury, saying the bank clerk who feeds his children the unlawful is to be pitied, relating by sense that God cursed the eater of usury, the one who pays it, its writer and its two witnesses, who are equal in guilt, and saying usury is loss in this world and the next. He says Islamic banks were shaken less in the crisis of 2008 and hopes those who work in them are loyal to the idea and review their accounts. This is his opinion. The subject is connected to [[concept/riba|usury]]. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain, and his statement on Islamic banks and the crisis has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:09:30}
- He lists four things that give a person joy: manliness, fidelity, generosity and piety. He cites verses by Ahmad Shawqi on mercy and keeping covenants, tells that on the night of the Hijra the Prophet held deposits of Quraysh and ordered them returned to their owners, and says the generous are generous with money, feeling and affection. He mentions the garment of piety. See [[passage/quran-7-26|al-A'raf, verse 26]] and [[passage/quran-4-58|al-Nisa, verse 58]]. The subject is connected to [[concept/taqwa|piety]]. (needs_source: the attribution of the verses and the account of the deposits have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:14:00}
- He defines piety as that God not find you where He forbade you nor miss you where He commanded you, and credits [[person/ali-ibn-abi-talib|Ali]] with saying it is fear of the Majestic, acting by revelation, contentment with little and readiness for the day of departure. (needs_source: both definitions have no chain and the second is credited to Ali with no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:14:30}
- He lists four things that breed hatred: pride, envy, lying and tale-bearing. The proud are hated even by their wives because they accept no advice, pride is the way of Iblis, people fear the envious, a person should say what God willed, there is no power but with God on seeing a blessing, the liar seeks out lies, and the tale-bearer sets people against each other. See [[passage/quran-7-12|al-A'raf, verse 12]] and [[passage/quran-18-39|al-Kahf, verse 39]]. The subject is connected to [[concept/kadhib|lying]] and [[concept/namimah|tale-bearing]]. This is his opinion. (needs_source: his saying that the liar seeks out lies is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:17:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:19:30}
- He lists four things that bring provision: night prayer in the last third of the night when God descends to the lowest heaven, seeking forgiveness, charity, and much prayer and remembrance. He says Ali told one who complained of lack of rain, one who complained of childlessness and one who complained of narrow means each to seek forgiveness often, and recalls Noah's words to his people. See [[passage/quran-71-10|Nuh, verse 10]], [[passage/quran-71-11|Nuh, verse 11]] and [[passage/quran-71-12|Nuh, verse 12]]. The subject is connected to [[practice/qiyam-al-layl|night prayer]] and [[concept/istighfar|seeking forgiveness]]. (needs_source: the account credited to Ali and the report are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:20:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:23:00}
- He lists four things that block provision: sleeping after sunrise, neglecting prayer, laziness and betraying the trust by divulging what was said in gatherings. He relates by sense that gatherings are by trust, says it is betrayal for a wife to tell her friends of her husband's stinginess and harshness or for a husband to tell his family his wife's secret, and reads the betrayal of the wives of Noah and Lot as taking out the secret of their households. See [[passage/quran-66-10|al-Tahrim, verse 10]]. The subject is connected to [[person/nuh|Noah]] and [[person/lut|Lot]]. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain, and the reading of the betrayal is his own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:26:30}
- He tells that a billionaire took his son to a poor village on the edge of the city and the boy said they have ten dogs and we have one, they grow their own food and guard one another while we buy ours and guard ours with companies, and concluded that it is we who are poor. He says failure to seek refuge from the whisperings of the devils breeds worries. (needs_source: the story has no chain and names no one) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:26:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:29:30}
- He says the scholars said beware of six, meaning six kinds of women: the one who moans and is pessimistic, the one who yearns for her former husband, the one who reminds her husband of her wealth, the one who covets everything she sees, the one preoccupied with adornment and imitating unbelieving women in dress, and the one who talks too much. He says this is a trial God places on whom He wills. This is his statement. (needs_source: the scholars are not named, and the description is his own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:31:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:32:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:33:30}
- He relates by sense that Satan shares a person's food if he does not mention God's name, and that when a believer enters his house, names God and greets his family the devils say you have no lodging or supper tonight, but if he does not they say you have found lodging and supper. He explains that God hears the whisperings that we do not, so we seek refuge in Him. See [[passage/quran-23-97|al-Mu'minun, verse 97]]. (needs_source: both reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep24 00:35:30}

## Episode 25: set your face to the religion as one upright, and ignorance as a state and not a period
- He pauses at the two verses of Surat Yunus that command setting one's face to the religion as one upright, not being among the polytheists and not calling on what neither benefits nor harms besides God, whoever does so being among the wrongdoers. He says it is an honour of this community that it is included in the call addressed to the Prophet, so we are commanded and forbidden with him. See [[passage/quran-10-105|Yunus, verse 105]] and [[passage/quran-10-106|Yunus, verse 106]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:01:00}
- He compares God's choice of Moses for His messages and speech with His choice of Muhammad, says the upright religion has no murkiness or riddles and that God placed no hardship in it, and relates by sense that the Prophet said we were sent to make things easy and not hard and that gentleness adorns whatever it is in. See [[passage/quran-7-144|al-A'raf, verse 144]] and [[passage/quran-22-78|al-Hajj, verse 78]]. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:02:30}
- He says schools teach that the age of ignorance is a period before Islam and that pre-Islamic poetry is the seven suspended odes, and holds that ignorance is a state of separation of the creature from the Creator, of people from the Lord of people and of the earth from the teachings of heaven, so that there is a Roman, a Pharaonic and a Greek ignorance, and any civilisation cut off from God's teachings is ignorance even if its people reach the moon. This is his opinion. The subject is connected to [[concept/shirk|polytheism]]. (needs_source: his description of what schools teach has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:05:30}
- He says Freud and others held that the family was not made by God but is a social idea that may dissolve, that the materialist and dialectical reading of history holds that buildings, civilisation, seas and clouds were made by nature, and that these ideas are taught in philosophy and sociology departments. This is his view of those schools. (needs_source: the views credited to Freud and others have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:06:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:13:00}
- He says the medieval church stood against Darwin, who called it backward while it called him an unbeliever and a heretic, so religion and science parted in Europe, unlike Islam in which knowledge occurs hundreds of times, and says the root for knowledge occurs eight hundred and sixty-five times, the verses on acts of worship are a hundred and ten, and the verses of the Qur'an are sixty-two times that. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the counts have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:08:30}
- He explains the order in Surat al-Rahman as the Qur'an mentioning teaching before creation and then teaching again, so that a human being is created between two teachings, and holds that one who does not learn the Qur'an and its exposition is as if he did not exist. This is his explanation. See [[passage/quran-55-1|al-Rahman, verse 1]], [[passage/quran-55-2|al-Rahman, verse 2]], [[passage/quran-55-3|al-Rahman, verse 3]] and [[passage/quran-55-4|al-Rahman, verse 4]]. (needs_source: the interpretation is his own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:09:30}
- He says Darwin's theory has been scientifically shown to fail, that Europeans no longer teach it to their children while we insist on teaching it, that the church punished Galileo, and that one who held the earth round declared it as he died. This is his opinion. (needs_source: his statements on the theory, Galileo and the other account have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:11:30}
- He relates that when God willed to create Adam He took a handful from the regions of the earth so that people came in the measure of the earth they came from, some like sand, some like clay and some like gold which grows stronger when tried, and says every person has faculties God placed in him and a natural disposition that hates wrong and loves justice. The subject is connected to [[concept/fitra|the natural disposition]]. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain, and the comparison with sand, clay and gold is his own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:13:00}
- He says guidance and error have existed since Adam and his two sons were created, that ignorance and faith existed from the start, that a Muslim has clear standards, and that Islam knows no inherited sin. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-2-36|al-Baqara, verse 36]] and [[passage/quran-17-70|al-Isra, verse 70]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:15:00}
- He says each person bears his own fault except in the hereafter, where, in his words, a woman who goes out in display and her guardian are held to account, and tells that a young man wanted to propose to a girl and Ibn Abbas advised against it because she looks at men and celebrities. He says the believer is jealous because God is jealous and has forbidden indecencies, outward and inward, and gives the example of animal jealousy such as the male kangaroo. See [[passage/quran-7-33|al-A'raf, verse 33]]. (needs_source: the story of Ibn Abbas has no chain, what he says of animals has no source, and the first sentence is garbled in the transcript) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:18:00}
- He tells that a man bet his friend at a zoo in Moscow that he could lay his hand on a tigress's head and she ate his hand, and offers it as an illustration of jealousy. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:18:30}
- He mentions Umm Jamil and Abu Lahab, saying God described Umm Jamil as the carrier of firewood and that Abu Lahab's name was Abd al-Uzza, and says we judge no one an unbeliever unless he died in disbelief or declared it openly. See [[passage/quran-111-1|al-Masad, verse 1]]. The subject is connected to [[person/umm-jamil|Umm Jamil]], [[person/abu-lahab|Abu Lahab]] and [[person/abu-jahl|Abu Jahl]]. (needs_source: Abu Lahab's name and the description of his face have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:18:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:20:00}
- He says a Muslim should make the details of his life part of religion and not evade in asking for rulings, wonders whether Abu Bakr, Umar and Bilal would have gone if invited to a wedding, and says one who evades deceives only himself. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-2-9|al-Baqara, verse 9]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:21:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:22:30}
- He says polytheism is of two kinds, the polytheism of old ignorance and that of modern civilisations. He tells that [[person/umar-ibn-al-khattab|Umar]] said he worshipped in ignorance a god of dates and ate it when hungry, that the Prophet asked whether they had minds and he answered they had minds but no guidance, and speaks of an astronaut who said he found no lord in space. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the account of Umar, the exchange with the Prophet and the astronaut story have no chain and name no one) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:25:30}
- He says modern ignorance has large institutions, among them Freemasonry, that want to take people away from God's religion, that the global order wants to unify cultures, and that God forbade calling on any other besides Him who neither benefits nor harms. This is his opinion. (needs_source: his description of the institutions and Freemasonry has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep25 00:28:30}

## Episode 26: our Lord, let not our hearts deviate after You have guided us
- He pauses at the supplication in Surat Al Imran that God not let hearts swerve after guidance and grant mercy, and that He will gather people for a day about which there is no doubt, never failing His promise. He says it is not necessary to put out other people's lights in order for one's own to show. See [[passage/quran-3-8|Al Imran, verse 8]] and [[passage/quran-3-9|Al Imran, verse 9]]. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:02:00}
- He relates by sense that when the Prophet was asked who is the shrewdest of people he said those who most remember death and are best prepared for it, and that such are the shrewd who carried off the honour of this world and the next. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:02:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:03:00}
- He says seven matters, once they enter the heart, make it swerve after guidance, and lists those the transcript preserves: harm, belittling, mockery, following desire, bad character and the quarrelsome adversary, saying a seventh is unreadable. This is his enumeration. (needs_source: the seven are his own list and the seventh cannot be read) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:07:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:08:00}
- He relates by sense the report of the woman who entered the fire over a cat she confined, neither feeding it nor letting it eat the creatures of the earth, says there is not one Muslim among the billion and a half who does not know this report, and says causing harm to people makes one swerve after guidance. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain, and the number has no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:04:00}
- He mentions that the Prophet rebuked [[person/usama-ibn-zayd|Usama]] for killing a man in battle who had pronounced the testimony, Usama saying he said it for fear of the sword and the Prophet answering did you split open his heart, and concludes that people are not to be judged by intentions nor is one to claim to know what is in hearts. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:05:30}
- He says following desire is a calamity, that a woman who prays and gives charity yet does not veil is following her desire on this matter, that some present theses in sharia faculties to prove that the headscarf is not an obligation, and that one who does not wish to apply God's law should keep it inside himself and not publicise it. This is his opinion. The subject is connected to [[concept/hawa|desire]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:09:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:09:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:10:30}
- He says following desire brings a fall, and recalls God's words about one who takes his desire as his god and whom God leads astray knowingly. See [[passage/quran-45-23|al-Jathiya, verse 23]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:10:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:10:30}
- He relates by sense that the heaviest thing put in a servant's scale on the Day of Resurrection is good character, that Umm Salama asked the Prophet about a woman who marries two husbands in this world and was told she will be with the one of better character, and that the nearest to him in seat on that day are the best in character, those easy to approach who befriend and are befriended. The subject is connected to [[person/umm-salama|Umm Salama]] and [[concept/khuluq|character]]. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:11:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:12:30}
- He says the quarrelsome adversary is one who loves disputation and distorting people's image, that God commanded justice even toward those we dislike, that justice is the foundation of rule and that injustice portends the ruin of nations, attributing this to [[person/ibn-khaldun|Ibn Khaldun]], and credits Ibn Taymiyya with saying that God supports a just state even if unbelieving. The subject is connected to [[concept/adl|justice]]. (needs_source: the sayings are credited to Ibn Khaldun and Ibn Taymiyya with no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:13:30}
- He says some people gain nothing from a scholar, preacher or Friday speaker, for when verses on the wrongdoers, the transgressors and the hypocrites are read to them they say it is not us, and that guidance, steadfastness and swerving all lie in the heart, which is why God commanded the supplication. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-67-10|al-Mulk, verse 10]]. The subject is connected to [[concept/hidaya|guidance]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:14:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:15:00}
- He explains that the mercy sought in the supplication is to persist in guidance and steadfastness and not be snatched away by the devils of mankind and jinn, that God the Giver gives much for little, and relates by sense that a servant may utter a word to which he gives no thought that drops him into the fire or raises him to God's good pleasure. He explains the difference between a phrase and a word as a phrase crossing from one bank to the other. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain, and the derivation of the word is his own) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:15:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:17:00}
- He insists that every person must be certain he will be gathered with creation on the Day of Resurrection for reckoning, so that no wrong of a wrongdoer, killer, backbiter, liar or briber is lost. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:17:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:18:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:18:30}
- He relates by sense that a woman came to ask the Prophet and sat beside him and asked about the farewell pilgrimage, and an unmarried young man came and the Prophet moved him to the other side, and draws from it his view on mixing of the sexes. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain, and the transcript is garbled) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:20:30}
- He says Ramadan is a charge for recounting one's own accounts, that God does not fail His promise and provision is decreed so one need not flatter, mentions that God provided Jonah a whale to swallow him in order to save him and that the fire became cool and safe for Abraham. See [[passage/quran-21-69|al-Anbiya, verse 69]]. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:24:00}
- He tells of a calm man on a ship with his nervous wife as the waves rose and she began to scream, so he lifted a knife and asked whether it occurred to her that he would kill her, and she said no, because you love me and harm does not come from one who loves, to which he answered that he knew the owner of the ship loves us so no harm will come from him and he will save us. He says God sent us down into this universe in love. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:25:30}
- He relates that the universe is in revolt against the son of Adam who ate God's provision and did not give thanks, so the sea asks to drown him, the earth to shake him and the sky to drop a fragment, and God asks His creatures whether they created them, they say no, and He says leave Me and My servants, whoever repents I am his beloved and whoever does not I am his physician and I am more merciful to them than a mother to her children. He says al-Wahhab is the giver of life, growth, victory, honour, health and covering. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:25:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:26:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:26:30}
- He says the believers who planted good in this world will not be grieved by the greatest terror and the angels will receive them, that contentment with God includes not recounting one's calamity out of reverence, and quotes a saying that if you wish to witness His giving look at what comes from Him to you and if you wish to witness His mercy and covering look at what comes from you to Him. See [[passage/quran-21-103|al-Anbiya, verse 103]]. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the quoted saying is unattributed) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:28:30}
- He says parents give their children everything and some children give no thanks, and tells of a son who put his elderly father out of the house and the father said he had done the same to his own father forty years before, adding that an old man who grows weak and forgets names does not forget you. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:29:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep26 00:31:00}

## Episode 27: the etiquette of dialogue with the other
- He pauses at the verse of Surat Al Imran inviting the People of the Book to a common word, that we worship none but God, associate nothing with Him and take none of us as lords besides God. He says we have lost the language of dialogue among ourselves before we lost it with the other, and recalls God's command to call with wisdom and good counsel and to argue in the best way. See [[passage/quran-3-64|Al Imran, verse 64]] and [[passage/quran-16-125|al-Nahl, verse 125]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:00:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:01:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:01:30}
- He says Surat al-Kafirun draws the line of separation without calling their religion false, trivial or foolish and without calling the Muslims' better, which is a courtesy that opens the other's heart, and that the Qur'an says you will not be asked about what we committed nor we about what you do. This is his explanation. See [[passage/quran-109-6|al-Kafirun, verse 6]] and [[passage/quran-34-25|Saba, verse 25]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:01:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:02:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:02:30}
- He says the West erred in ascribing to Jesus the saying render to Caesar what is Caesar's and to God what is God's, since everything belongs to God, so building, teaching children, marrying off daughters, visiting the sick and following a funeral are all works for the hereafter when done for God's sake. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the saying is ascribed to Jesus with no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:03:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:03:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:04:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:04:30}
- He addresses one who wishes to take off the cloak of Islam and walk bare under the banner of ignorance, saying a Muslim has distinguishing traits and God honoured the children of Adam. He tells that Umar objected at the treaty of al-Hudaybiyya to what looked like acceptance of the terms, that Abu Bakr sat at the tent door, and that when Suhayl ibn Amr reached for the Prophet's beard one of those present struck his hand. See [[event/treaty-of-hudaybiyya|the treaty of al-Hudaybiyya]] and [[person/suhayl-ibn-amr|Suhayl ibn Amr]]. (needs_source: the name of the one who struck the hand and the other details have no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:04:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:05:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:05:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:06:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:06:30}
- He tells that [[person/abu-sufyan-ibn-harb|Abu Sufyan]] came to Medina to renew the treaty and went in to his daughter [[person/umm-habiba-bint-abi-sufyan|Umm Habiba]], the Prophet's wife, and when he went to sit on the Prophet's bedding she folded it and said he was impure and it was the Prophet's pure bedding, from which he draws that the lawful and the forbidden are clear. (needs_source: the story has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:07:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:08:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:08:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:09:00}
- He says religion with God is one and God charged the prophets to establish religion and not split over it, relates by sense that the Prophet likened himself and the prophets before him to a house completed but for the place of one brick, which is he, and says dialogue takes place in the space of shared values since no one approves injustice, killing, dictatorship, occupation of lands or attacks on sanctities. See [[passage/quran-42-13|al-Shura, verse 13]]. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:10:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:11:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:11:30}
- He says one who cannot hold a dialogue with his wife fails at making peace with another state, advises beginning with good and praise even with a small child and avoiding conflict, and notes that God joined hearts. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-8-63|al-Anfal, verse 63]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:12:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:12:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:13:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:13:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:14:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:14:30}
- He relates that when Aisha had angered the Prophet he asked her whom she wanted as arbiter between them and she chose her father Abu Bakr, who on judging raised his hand and struck her until her nose bled, so she sheltered behind the Prophet, who told Abu Bakr they had brought him as arbiter and not to strike, and who would remind Aisha of this whenever she was angry. He cites the verse on arbiters between spouses. See [[passage/quran-4-35|al-Nisa, verse 35]]. The subject is connected to [[person/aisha|Aisha]]. (needs_source: the report has no chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:15:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:16:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:16:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:17:00}
- He sets out Abraham's dialogue with his father, saying he addressed him with O my father and excused himself by saying knowledge had come to him that had not come to his father, contrasts our children who say to their fathers I am learned and you are ignorant, and says Abraham was the height of courtesy and greeted his father with peace even when threatened. See [[passage/quran-19-43|Maryam, verse 43]] and [[passage/quran-19-47|Maryam, verse 47]]. This is his explanation. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:19:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:19:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:20:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:20:30}
- He says courtesy in dialogue is by proof and logic without belittling the other, relates that Umar passed a people who had lit a fire and called peace upon you people of the light and not people of the fire, and tells that [[person/utba-ibn-rabia|Utba ibn Rabia]] offered the Prophet wealth, kingship and physicians and that when the Prophet recited the verses of Fussilat to him he prostrated and returned to his people with a changed face saying it was neither poetry nor soothsaying. See [[passage/quran-41-1|Fussilat, verse 1]]. (needs_source: the reports on Umar and Utba are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:21:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:22:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:22:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:23:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:23:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:24:00}
- He mentions the stance of [[person/al-walid-ibn-al-mughira|al-Walid ibn al-Mughira]] toward the Qur'an, that he thought and measured, frowned and scowled, then said this is only magic handed down. See [[passage/quran-74-18|al-Muddaththir, verse 18]], [[passage/quran-74-24|al-Muddaththir, verse 24]] and [[passage/quran-74-25|al-Muddaththir, verse 25]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:24:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:24:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:25:00}
- He quotes [[person/muhammad-al-ghazali-modern|Shaykh Muhammad al-Ghazali]] as saying Islam is a precious commodity promoted by a failed trader, and credits Umar with saying he complains of the vigour of the wicked and the weakness of the trustworthy. (needs_source: both sayings have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:25:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:25:30}
- He says Muslims believe in the Torah God sent down on Moses without distortion, in the Gospel, and in Moses, Jesus, Noah and Joseph, that disbelief in Moses and the Torah sent down on him is disbelief in Muhammad and the Qur'an, and that they make no distinction between any of the messengers. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-2-285|al-Baqara, verse 285]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:27:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:27:30}
- He says Islam was carried to victory by the Arabs Abu Bakr and Umar, the Indian Muhammad Iqbal, Suhayb the Roman, Salman the Persian, Bilal the Abyssinian and Tariq ibn Ziyad the Berber, so it should not be confined to one nationality. This is his opinion. The subject is connected to [[person/salman-al-farisi|Salman al-Farisi]], [[person/suhayb-ibn-sinan|Suhayb]] and [[person/tariq-ibn-ziyad|Tariq ibn Ziyad]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:27:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:28:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:28:30}
- He says dialogue with the other requires understanding the ground on which he stands and finding his key, that our brothers in humanity in the West understood what we did not, that some of the devout understood the religion wrongly, divorced the world and others married it, and that a Muslim should build the world as God commanded. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:28:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:29:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:29:30}
- He likens Satan to a transmitting station and the person to a receiver he should switch off, says the Qur'an, scholars and preachers are also stations to receive from, and says the hardest thing he met in calling to God over more than forty years is resistance to information. This is his opinion. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:30:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:30:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:31:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:31:30}
- He says some accept what Marx, Keynes, Adam Smith and Freud said without checking, yet ask whether a report from Abu Hurayra is sound, and gives the report religion is dealing with others as from Abu Hurayra and says it was queried. (needs_source: the report is given with no chain and no grading, and the views credited to the thinkers have no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:32:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:32:30}
- He says Islam does not ask one to leave one's mind outside the mosque but one's shoes, that it charges only the mind, relates by sense that the pen is lifted from the child, the insane and the sleeper, and likens the mind to a parachute that works only when opened. This is his opinion. (needs_source: the report is by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:33:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:33:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:34:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:34:30}
- He says religion is taken whole and not in parts, giving the example of a woman who prays and fasts yet rejects the headscarf, and mentions the Children of Israel and the story of the Sabbath, their trick of setting nets before it and gathering them after. This is his opinion. See [[passage/quran-2-85|al-Baqara, verse 85]] and [[passage/quran-7-163|al-A'raf, verse 163]]. {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:34:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:35:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:35:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:36:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:36:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:37:00}
- He says zakat is not given to one's son, daughter, parents or wife, and that a wealthy wife may give her poor husband from the zakat of her wealth. This is his opinion. The subject is connected to [[concept/zakat|zakat]]. (needs_source: both rulings are given with no school and no source) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:37:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:37:30}
- He urges the good word and not reminding others of what one gave, notes that God rebukes those who count their conversion as a favour, says whoever does a kindness should forget it and whoever receives one should not, and relates by sense that one who does not thank people does not thank God and that if you cannot repay a kindness you should pray for the giver. See [[passage/quran-49-17|al-Hujurat, verse 17]]. (needs_source: the reports are by sense and without a chain) {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:38:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:38:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:39:00} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:39:30} {cite:assabile-omar-abdelkafy mawkib-al-nour/ep27 00:40:00}
